1  # From bus (0-indexed)\n    t_bus = branch_data[:, 1].astype(int) - 1  # To bus (0-indexed)\n    r = branch_data[:, 2]  # Resistance (pu)\n    x = branch_data[:, 3]  # Reactance (pu)\n    b = branch_data[:, 4]  # Total line charging (pu)\n    rate_a = branch_data[:, 5] / baseMVA  # MVA rating (pu)\n    tap = branch_data[:, 8]  # Transformer tap ratio\n    shift = branch_data[:, 9] * np.pi / 180  # Phase shift (radians)\n    branch_status = branch_data[:, 10].astype(int)\n\n    # Set default tap ratio and shift for lines (non-transformers)\n    tap[tap == 0] = 1.0\n\n    # Compute branch admittances\n    z = r + 1j * x\n    y = 1.0 / z\n    y[~np.isfinite(y)] = 0  # Handle zero impedance\n\n    # Transformer complex ratio\n    tau = tap * np.exp(1j * shift)\n\n    # Build incidence matrices for generator and load locations\n    gen_at_bus = [[] for _ in range(n_bus)]\n    for i, gb in enumerate(gen_bus):\n        if gen_status[i] == 1:\n            gen_at_bus[gb].append(i)\n\n    # Initialize CasADi optimization\n    opti = Opti()\n\n    # Decision variables\n    # Complex voltage at each bus: V = Vr + j*Vi\n    Vr = opti.variable(n_bus)\n    Vi = opti.variable(n_bus)\n\n    # Generator real and reactive power\n    Pg = opti.variable(n_gen)\n    Qg = opti.variable(n_gen)\n\n    # Objective: minimize generation cost\n    obj = 0\n    for i in range(n_gen):\n        if gen_status[i] == 1:\n            obj += c2[i] * Pg[i]**2 + c1[i] * Pg[i] + c0[i]\n\n    opti.minimize(obj)\n\n    # Constraints\n\n    # 1. Reference bus angle constraint\n    opti.subject_to(Vi[ref_bus] == 0)\n\n    # 2. Power balance at each bus (nodal balance)\n    for i in range(n_bus):\n        # Generator injection at this bus\n        Pg_bus = sum([Pg[k] for k in gen_at_bus[i]]) if gen_at_bus[i] else 0\n        Qg_bus = sum([Qg[k] for k in gen_at_bus[i]]) if gen_at_bus[i] else 0\n\n        # Shunt injection (absorbed power)\n        Vm_sq = Vr[i]**2 + Vi[i]**2\n        Psh = Gs[i] * Vm_sq\n        Qsh = -Bs[i] * Vm_sq  # Negative because capacitive shunt generates Q\n\n        # Branch flows from this bus\n        P_branch = 0\n        Q_branch = 0\n\n        # Flows on branches where i is the \"from\" bus\n        for k in range(n_branch):\n            if branch_status[k] == 1 and f_bus[k] == i:\n                j = t_bus[k]\n                # Pi-section model: from-side\n                # Series admittance\n                y_series = y[k]\n                g_series = y_series.real\n                b_series = y_series.imag\n\n                # Shunt admittance at from side\n                g_shunt_f = 0\n                b_shunt_f = b[k] / 2\n\n                # Voltage at from and to buses\n                Vr_f = Vr[i]\n                Vi_f = Vi[i]\n                Vr_t = Vr[j]\n                Vi_t = Vi[j]\n\n                # Transformer ratio\n                tau_mag = abs(tau[k])\n                tau_ang = np.angle(tau[k])\n                tau_r = np.cos(tau_ang)\n                tau_i = np.sin(tau_ang)\n\n                # Power flow from i to j (complex power)\n                # S_ij = V_i * conj(I_ij)\n                # I_ij = (V_i/tau - V_j) * y_series + V_i/tau * y_shunt_f\n\n                # V_i/tau = (Vr_f + j*Vi_f) / (tau_mag * (tau_r + j*tau_i))\n                #         = (Vr_f + j*Vi_f) * conj(tau_r + j*tau_i) / tau_mag^2\n                Vf_tau_r = (Vr_f * tau_r + Vi_f * tau_i) / (tau_mag**2)\n                Vf_tau_i = (Vi_f * tau_r - Vr_f * tau_i) / (tau_mag**2)\n\n                # Current through series element: I_series = (V_i/tau - V_j) * y_series\n                I_series_r = (Vf_tau_r - Vr_t) * g_series - (Vf_tau_i - Vi_t) * b_series\n                I_series_i = (Vf_tau_r - Vr_t) * b_series + (Vf_tau_i - Vi_t) * g_series\n\n                # Current through shunt element: I_shunt = V_i/tau * y_shunt_f\n                I_shunt_r = Vf_tau_r * g_shunt_f - Vf_tau_i * b_shunt_f\n                I_shunt_i = Vf_tau_r * b_shunt_f + Vf_tau_i * g_shunt_f\n\n                # Total current from bus i\n                I_total_r = I_series_r + I_shunt_r\n                I_total_i = I_series_i + I_shunt_i\n\n                # Power S = V * conj(I)\n                P_ij = Vr_f * I_total_r + Vi_f * I_total_i\n                Q_ij = Vi_f * I_total_r - Vr_f * I_total_i\n\n                P_branch += P_ij\n                Q_branch += Q_ij\n\n        # Flows on branches where i is the \"to\" bus\n        for k in range(n_branch):\n            if branch_status[k] == 1 and t_bus[k] == i:\n                j = f_bus[k]\n                # Pi-section model: to-side\n                y_series = y[k]\n                g_series = y_series.real\n                b_series = y_series.imag\n\n                # Shunt admittance at to side\n                g_shunt_t = 0\n                b_shunt_t = b[k] / 2\n\n                # Voltage at from and to buses\n                Vr_f = Vr[j]\n                Vi_f = Vi[j]\n                Vr_t = Vr[i]\n                Vi_t = Vi[i]\n\n                # Transformer ratio\n                tau_mag = abs(tau[k])\n                tau_ang = np.angle(tau[k])\n                tau_r = np.cos(tau_ang)\n                tau_i = np.sin(tau_ang)\n\n                # V_f/tau\n                Vf_tau_r = (Vr_f * tau_r + Vi_f * tau_i) / (tau_mag**2)\n                Vf_tau_i = (Vi_f * tau_r - Vr_f * tau_i) / (tau_mag**2)\n\n                # Current into bus i (to side): I_ji = (V_j - V_i/tau) * y_series + V_j * y_shunt_t\n                I_series_r = (Vr_t - Vf_tau_r) * g_series - (Vi_t - Vr_t * I_total_i\n\n                P_branch += P_ji\n                Q_branch += Q_ji\n\n        # Power balance: Generation - Demand - Shunt = Branch flows\n        opti.subject_to(Pg_bus - Pd[i] - Load network data\n    data = load_network_data(network_file)\n    baseMVA = data['baseMVA']\n\n    # Parse bus data\n    bus_data = np.array(data['bus'])\n    n_bus = bus_data.shape[0]\n    bus_id = bus_data[:, 0].astype(int)\n    bus_type = bus_data[:, 1].astype(int)\n    Pd = bus_data[:, 2] / baseMVA  # Real power demand (pu)\n    Qd = bus_data[:, 3] / baseMVA  # Reactive power demand (pu)\n    Gs = bus_data[:, 4]  # Shunt conductance (pu)\n    Bs = bus_data[:, 5]  # Shunt susceptance (pu)\n    Vm_init = bus_data[:, 7]  # Initial voltage magnitude\n    Va_init = bus_data[:, 8] * np.pi / 180  # Initial voltage angle (radians)\n    Vmin = bus_data[:, 12]  # Min voltage (pu)\n    Vmax = bus_data[:, 11]  # Max voltage (pu)\n\n    # Find reference bus (type 3)\n    ref_bus = np.where(bus_type == 3)[0]\n    if len(ref_bus) == 0:\n        ref_bus = [0]  # Default to first bus if no slack bus\n    ref_bus = ref_bus[0]\n\n    # Parse generator data\n    gen_data = np.array(data['gen'])\n    n_gen = gen_data.shape[0]\n    gen_bus = gen_data[:, 0].astype(int) - 1  # Convert to 0-indexed\n    Pg_init = gen_data[:, 1] / baseMVA\n    Qg_init = gen_data[:, 2] / baseMVA\n    Qmax = gen_data[:, 3] / baseMVA\n    Qmin = gen_data[:, 4] / baseMVA\n    gen_status = gen_data[:, 7].astype(int)\n    Pmax = gen_data[:, 8] / baseMVA\n    Pmin = gen_data[:, 9] / baseMVA\n\n    # Parse cost data\n    gencost_data = np.array(data['gencost'])\n    cost_model = gencost_data[:, 0].astype(int)\n    ncost = gencost_data[:, 3].astype(int)\n\n    # Build cost coefficients (assuming quadratic cost: c2*P^2 + c1*P + c0)\n    c2 = np.zeros(n_gen)\n    c1 = np.zeros(n_gen)\n    c0 = np.zeros(n_gen)\n\n    for i in range(n_gen):\n        if cost_model[i] == 2:  # Polynomial cost\n            if ncost[i] >= 1:\n                c0[i] = gencost_data[i, 4 + ncost[i] - 1]\n            if ncost[i] >= 2:\n                c1[i] = gencost_data[i, 4 + ncost[i] - 2]\n            if ncost[i] >= 3:\n                c2[i] = gencost_data[i, 4 + ncost[i] - 3]\n\n    # Scale cost coefficients to per-unit\n    c2 = c2 * baseMVA * baseMVA\n    c1 = c1 * baseMVA\n\n    # Parse branch data\n    branch_data = np.array(data['branch'])\n    n_branch = branch_data.shape[0]\n    f_bus = branch_data[:, 0].astype(int) - 1  # From bus (0-indexed)\n    t_bus = branch_data[:, 1].astype(int) - 1  # To bus (0-indexed)\n    r = branch_data[:, 2]  # Resistance (pu)\n    x = branch_data[:, 3]  # Reactance (pu)\n    b = branch_data[:, 4]  # Total line charging (pu)\n    rate_a = branch_data[:, 5] / baseMVA  # MVA rating (pu)\n    tap = branch_data[:, 8]  # Transformer tap ratio\n    shift = branch_data[:, 9] * np.pi / 180  # Phase shift (radians)\n    branch_status = branch_data[:, 10].astype(int)\n\n    # Set default tap ratio and shift for lines (non-transformers)\n    tap[tap == 0] = 1.0\n\n    # Compute branch admittances\n    z = r + 1j * x\n    y = 1.0 / z\n    y[~np.isfinite(y)] = 0  # Handle zero impedance\n\n    # Transformer complex ratio\n    tau = tap * np.exp(1j * shift)\n\n    # Build incidence matrices for generator and load locations\n    gen_at_bus = [[] for _ in range(n_bus)]\n    for i, gb in enumerate(gen_bus):\n        if gen_status[i] == 1:\n            gen_at_bus[gb].append(i)\n\n    # Initialize CasADi optimization\n    opti = Opti()\n\n    # Decision variables\n    # Complex voltage at each bus: V = Vr + j*Vi\n    Vr = opti.variable(n_bus)\n    Vi = opti.variable(n_bus)\n\n    # Generator real and reactive power\n    Pg = opti.variable(n_gen)\n    Qg = opti.variable(n_gen)\n\n    # Objective: minimize generation cost\n    obj = 0\n    for i in range(n_gen):\n        if gen_status[i] == 1:\n            obj += c2[i] * Pg[i]**2 + c1[i] * Pg[i] + c0[i]\n\n    opti.minimize(obj)\n\n    # Constraints\n\n    # 1. Reference bus angle constraint\n    opti.subject_to(Vi[ref_bus] == 0)\n\n    # 2. Generator limits\n    for i in range(n_gen):\n        if gen_status[i] == 1:\n            opti.subject_to(Pg[i] >= Pmin[i])\n            opti.subject_to(Pg[i] <= Pmax[i])\n            opti.subject_to(Qg[i] >= Qmin[i])\n            opti.subject_to(Qg[i] <= Qmax[i])\n        else:\n            opti.subject_to(Pg[i] == 0)\n            opti.subject_to(Qg[i] == 0)\n\n    # 3. Voltage magnitude limits\n    for i in range(n_bus):\n        Vm_sq = Vr[i]**2 + Vi[i]**2\n        opti.subject_to(Vm_sq >= Vmin[i]**2)\n        opti.subject_to(Vm_sq <= Vmax[i]**2)\n\n    # 4. Power balance at each bus (nodal balance)\n    for i in range(n_bus):\n        # Generator injection at this bus\n        Pg_bus = sum([Pg[k] for k in gen_at_bus[i]]) if gen_at_bus[i] else 0\n        Qg_bus = sum([Qg[k] for k in gen_at_bus[i]]) if gen_at_bus[i] else 0\n\n        # Shunt injection (absorbed power)\n        Vm_sq = Vr[i]**2 + Vi[i]**2\n        Psh = Gs[i] * Vm_sq\n        Qsh = -Bs[i] * Vm_sq  # Negative because capacitive shunt generates Q\n\n        # Branch flows from this bus\n        P_branch = 0\n        Q_branch = 0\n\n        # Flows on branches where i is the \"from\" bus\n        for k in range(n_branch):\n            if branch_status[k] == 1 and f_bus[k] == i:\n                j = t_bus[k]\n                # Pi-section model: from-side\n                # Series admittance\n                y_series = y[k]\n                g_series = y_series.real\n                b_series = y_series.imag\n\n                # Shunt admittance at from side\n                g_shunt_f = 0\n                b_shunt_f = b[k] / 2\n\n                # Voltage at from and to buses\n                Vr_f = Vr[i]\n                Vi_f = Vi[i]\n                Vr_t = Vr[j]\n                Vi_t = Vi[j]\n\n                # Transformer ratio\n                tau_mag = abs(tau[k])\n                tau_ang = np.angle(tau[k])\n                tau_r = np.cos(tau_ang)\n                tau_i = np.sin(tau_ang)\n\n                # Power flow from i to j (complex power)\n                # S_ij = V_i * conj(I_ij)\n                # I_ij = (V_i/tau - V_j) * y_series + V_i/tau * y_shunt_f\n\n                # V_i/tau = (Vr_f + j*Vi_f) / (tau_mag * (tau_r + j*tau_i))\n                #         = (Vr_f + j*Vi_f) * conj(tau_r + j*tau_i) / tau_mag^2\n                Vf_tau_r = (Vr_f * tau_r + Vi_f * tau_i) / (tau_mag**2)\n                Vf_tau_i = (Vi_f * tau_r - Vr_f * tau_i) / (tau_mag**2)\n\n                # Current through series element: I_series = (V_i/tau - V_j) * y_series\n                I_series_r = (Vf_tau_r - Vr_t) * g_series - (Vf_tau_i - Vi_t) * b_series\n                I_series_i = (Vf_tau_r - Vr_t) * b_series + (Vf_tau_i - Vi_t) * g_series\n\n                # Current through shunt element: I_shunt = V_i/tau * y_shunt_f\n                I_shunt_r = Vf_tau_r * g_shunt_f - Vf_tau_i * b_shunt_f\n                I_shunt_i = Vf_tau_r * b_shunt_f + Vf_tau_i * g_shunt_f\n\n                # Total current from bus i\n                I_total_r = I_series_r + I_shunt_r\n                I_total_i = I_series_i + I_shunt_i\n\n                # Power S = V * conj(I)\n                P_ij = Vr_f * I_total_r + Vi_f * I_total_i\n                Q_ij = Vi_f * I_total_r - Vr_f * I_total_i\n\n                P_branch += P_ij\n                Q_branch += Q_ij\n\n        # Flows on branches where i is the \"to\" bus\n        for k in range(n_branch):\n            if branch_status[k] == 1 and t_bus[k] == i:\n                j = f_bus[k]\n                # Pi-section model: to-side\n                y_series = y[k]\n                g_series = y_series.real\n                b_series = y_series.imag\n\n                # Shunt admittance at to side\n                g_shunt_t = 0\n                b_shunt_t = b[k] / 2\n\n                # Voltage at from and to buses\n                Vr_f = Vr[j]\n                Vi_f = Vi[j]\n                Vr_t = Vr[i]\n                Vi_t = Vi[i]\n\n                # Transformer ratio\n                tau_mag = abs(tau[k])\n                tau_ang = np.angle(tau[k])\n                tau_r = np.cos(tau_ang)\n                tau_i = np.sin(tau_ang)\n\n                # V_f/tau\n                Vf_tau_r = (Vr_f * tau_r + Vi_f * tau_i) / (tau_mag**2)\n                Vf_tau_i = (Vi_f * tau_r - Vr_f * tau_i) / (tau_mag**2)\n\n                # Current into bus i (to side): I_ji = (V_j - V_i/tau) * y_series + V_j * y_shunt_t\n                I_series_r = (Vr_t - Vf_tau_r) * g_series - (Vi_t - Vf_tau_i) * b_series\n                I_series_i = (Vr_t - Vf_tau_r) * b_series + (Vi_t - Vf_tau_i) * g_series\n\n                I_shunt_r = Vr_t * g_shunt_t - Vi_t * b_shunt_t\n                I_shunt_i = Vr_t * b_shunt_t + Vi_t * g_shunt_t\n\n                I_total_r = I_series_r + I_shunt_r\n                I_total_i = I_series_i + I_shunt_i\n\n                # Power S = V * conj(I)\n                P_ji = Vr_t * I_total_r + Vi_t * I_total_i\n                Q_ji = Vi_t * I_total_r - Vr_t * I_total_i\n\n                P_branch += P_ji\n                Q_branch += Q_ji\n\n        # Power balance: Generation - Demand - Shunt = Branch flows\n        opti.subject_to(Pg_bus - Pd[i] - Psh == P_branch)\n        opti.subject_to(Qg_bus - Qd[i] - Qsh == Q_branch)\n\n    # 5. Branch flow limits (thermal limits)\n    for k in range(n_branch):\n        if branch_status[k] == 1 and rate_a[k] > 0:\n            i = f_bus[k]\n            j = t_bus[k]\n\n            # From side flow\n            y_series = y[k]\n            g_series = y_series.real\n            b_series = y_series.imag\n            g_shunt_f = 0\n            b_shunt_f = b[k] / 2\n\n            Vr_f = Vr[i]\n            Vi_f = Vi[i]\n            Vr_t = Vr[j]\n            Vi_t = Vi[j]\n\n            tau_mag = abs(tau[k])\n            tau_ang = np.angle(tau[k])\n            tau_r = np.cos(tau_ang)\n            tau_i = np.sin(tau_ang)\n\n            Vf_tau_r = (Vr_f * tau_r + Vi_f * tau_i) / (tau_mag**2)\n            Vf_tau_i = (Vi_f * tau_r - Vr_f * tau_i) / (tau_mag**2)\n\n            I_series_r = (Vf_tau_r - Vr_t) * g_series - (Vf_tau_i - Vi_t) * b_series\n            I_series_i = (Vf_tau_r - Vr_t) * b_series + (Vf_tau_i - Vi_t) * g_series\n            I_shunt_r = Vf_tau_r * g_shunt_f - Vf_tau_i * b_shunt_f\n            I_shunt_i = Vf_tau_r * b_shunt_f + Vf_tau_i * g_shunt_f\n            I_total_r = I_series_r + I_shunt_r\n            I_total_i = I_series_i + I_shunt_i\n\n            P_ij = Vr_f * I_total_r + Vi_f * I_total_i\n            Q_ij = Vi_f * I_total_r - Vr_f * I_total_i\n\n            S_ij_sq = P_ij**2 + Q_ij**2\n            opti.subject_to(S_ij_sq <= rate_a[k]**2)\n\n            # From side flow\n        y_series = y[k]\n        g_series = y_series.real\n        b_series = y_series.imag\n        g_shunt_f = 0\n        b_shunt_f = b[k] / 2\n\n        Vr_f = Vr_sol[i]\n        Vi_f = Vi_sol[i]\n        Vr_t = Vr_sol[j]\n        Vi_t = Vi_sol[j]\n\n        tau_mag = abs(tau[k])\n        tau_ang = np.angle(tau[k])\n        tau_r = np.cos(tau_ang)\n        tau_i = np.sin(tau_ang)\n\n        Vf_tau_r = (Vr_f * tau_r + Vi_f * tau_i) / (tau_mag**2)\n        Vf_tau_i = (Vi_f * tau_r - Vr_f * tau_i) / (tau_mag**2)\n\n        I_series_r = (Vf_tau_r - Vr_t) * g_series - (Vf_tau_i - Vi_t) * b_series\n        I_series_i = (Vf_tau_r - Vr_t) * b_series + (Vf_tau_i - Vi_t) * g_series\n        I_shunt_r = Vf_tau_r * g_shunt_f - Vf_tau_i * b_shunt_f\n        I_shunt_i = Vf_tau_r * b_shunt_f + Vf_tau_i * g_shunt_f\n        I_total_r = I_series_r + I_shunt_r\n        I_total_i = I_series_i + I_shunt_i\n\n        P_ij = Vr_f * I_total_r + Vi_f * I_total_i\n        Q_ij = Vi_f * I_total_r - Vr_f * I_total_i\n        S_ij = np.sqrt(P_ij**2 + Q_ij**2)\n\n        # \\(P_{pu} = P_{MW} / baseMVA\\)\n  - \\(Q_{pu} = Q_{MVAr} / baseMVA\\)\n  - \\(|S|_{MVA} = |S|_{pu} \\cdot baseMVA\\)\n\n### Transformer handling (MATPOWER TAP + SHIFT)\n\n- Use \\(T_{ij} = tap \\cdot e^{j \\cdot shift}\\).\n- **Implementation shortcut (real tap + phase shift):**\n  - If `abs(TAP) < 1e-12`, treat `tap = 1.0` (no transformer).\n  - Convert `SHIFT` from degrees to radians.\n  - Use the angle shift by modifying the angle difference:\n    - \\(\\delta_{ij} = \\theta_i - \\theta_j - shift\\)\n    - \\(\\delta_{ji} = \\theta_j - \\theta_i + shift\\)\n\n### Series admittance\n\nGiven `BR_R = r`, `BR_X = x`:\n\n- If `r == 0 and x == 0`, set `g = 0`, `b = 0` (avoid divide-by-zero).\n- Else:\n  - \\(y = 1/(r + jx) = g + jb\\)\n  - \\(g = r/(r^2 + x^2)\\)\n  - \\(b = -x/(r^2 + x^2)\\)\n\n### Line charging susceptance\n\n- `BR_B` is the **total** line charging susceptance \\(b_c\\) (per unit).\n- Each end gets \\(b_c/2\\) in the standard pi model.\n\n## Power flow equations (use these exactly)\n\nLet:\n- \\(V_i = |V_i| e^{j\\theta_i}\\), \\(V_j = |V_j| e^{j\\theta_j}\\)\n- `tap` is real, `shift` is radians\n- `inv_t = 1/tap`, `inv_t2 = inv_t^2`\n\nThen the real/reactive power flow from i→j is:\n\n- \\(P_{ij} = g |V_i|^2 inv\\_t2 - |V_i||V_j| inv\\_t (g\\cos\\delta_{ij} + b\\sin\\delta_{ij})\\)\n- \\(Q_{ij} = -(b + b_c/2)|V_i|^2 inv\\_t2 - |V_i||V_j| inv\\_t (g\\sin\\delta_{ij} - b\\cos\\delta_{ij})\\)\n\nAnd from j→i is:\n\n- \\(P_{ji} = g |V_j|^2 - |V_i||V_j| inv\\_t (g\\cos\\delta_{ji} + b\\sin\\delta_{ji})\\)\n- \\(Q_{ji} = -(b + b_c/2)|V_j|^2 - |V_i||V_j| inv\\_t (g\\sin\\delta_{ji} - b\\cos\\delta_{ji})\\)\n\nCompute apparent power:\n\n- \\(|S_{ij}| = \\sqrt{P_{ij}^2 + Q_{ij}^2}\\)\n- \\(|S_{ji}| = \\sqrt{P_{ji}^2 + Q_{ji}^2}\\)\n\n## Common uses\n\n### Enforce MVA limits (rateA)\n\n- `RATE_A` is an MVA limit (may be 0 meaning “no limit”).\n- Enforce in both directions:\n  - \\(|S_{ij}| \\le RATE_A\\)\n  - \\(|S_{ji}| \\le RATE_A\\)\n\n### Compute branch loading %\n\nFor reporting “most loaded branches”:\n\n- `loading_pct = 100 * max(|S_ij|, |S_ji|) / RATE_A` if `RATE_A > 0`, else 0.\n\n### Aggregate bus injections for nodal balance\n\nTo build the branch flow sum for each bus \\(i\\):\n\n- Add \\(P_{ij}, Q_{ij}\\) to bus i\n- Add \\(P_{ji}, Q_{ji}\\) to bus j\n\nThis yields arrays `P_out[i]`, `Q_out[i]` such that the nodal balance can be written as:\n\n- \\(P^g - P^d - G^s|V|^2 = P_{out}\\)\n- \\(Q^g - Q^d + B^s|V|^2 = Q_{out}\\)\n\n## Sanity checks (fast debug)\n\n- With `SHIFT=0` and `TAP=1`, if \\(V_i = V_j\\) and \\(\\theta_i=\\theta_j\\), then \\(P_{ij}\\approx 0\\) and \\(P_{ji}\\approx 0\\) (lossless only if r=0).\n- For a pure transformer (`r=x=0`) you should not get meaningful flows; treat as `g=b=0` (no series element).\n"}]},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"b7f13945-e96c-4356-9ad8-3eeda2413537","uuid":"ef581fbb-ac5e-4edb-bad6-3319fe068530","isSynthetic":true}

Real power demand (pu)\n    Qd = bus_data[:, 3] / baseMVA  # Reactive power demand (pu)\n    Gs = bus_data[:, 4]  # Shunt conductance (pu)\n    Bs = bus_data[:, 5]  # Shunt susceptance (pu)\n    Vm_init = bus_data[:, 7]  # Generator real power\n    Qg = opti.variable(n_gen)  # Generator reactive power\n\n    # Objective: minimize generation cost\n    obj = 0\n    for i in range(n_gen):\n        if gen_status[i] == 1:\n            obj += c2[i] * Pg[i]**2 + c1[i] * Pg[i] + c0[i]\n\n    opti.minimize(obj)\n\n    # Va[j_idx] - shift[k]\n                c_ij = cos(delta_ij)\n                s_ij = sin(delta_ij)\n\n                P_ij = g[k] * Vm[i_idx]**2 * inv_t2 - Vm[i_idx] * Vm[j_idx] * inv_t * (g[k] * c_ij + b[k] * s_ij)\n                Q_ij = -(b[k] + bc[k] / 2.0) * Vm[i_idx]**2 * inv_t2 - Va[j_idx] - shift[k]\n                c_ij = cos(delta_ij)\n                s_ij = sin(delta_ij)\n\n                P_ij = g[k] * Vm[i_idx]**2 * inv_t2 - Vm[i_idx] * Vm[j_idx] * inv_t * (g[k] * c_ij + b[k] * s_ij)\n                Q_ij = -(b[k] + bc[k] / 2.0) * Vm[i_idx]**2 * inv_t2 - {"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"type":"text","text":"Base directory for this skill: /logs/agent/sessions/skills/casadi-ipopt-nlp\n\n# CasADi + IPOPT for Nonlinear Programming\n\nCasADi is a symbolic framework for nonlinear optimization. IPOPT is an interior-point solver for large-scale NLP.\n\n## Quick start (Linux)\n\n```bash\napt-get update -qq && apt-get install -y -qq libgfortran5\npip install numpy==1.26.4 casadi==3.6.7\n```\n\n## Building an NLP\n\n### 1. Decision variables\n\n```python\nimport casadi as ca\n\nn_bus, n_gen = 100, 20\nVm = ca.MX.sym(\"Vm\", n_bus)   # Voltage magnitudes\nVa = ca.MX.sym(\"Va\", n_bus)   # Voltage angles (radians)\nPg = ca.MX.sym(\"Pg\", n_gen)   # Real power\nQg = ca.MX.sym(\"Qg\", n_gen)   # Reactive power\n\n# Stack into single vector for solver\nx = ca.vertcat(Vm, Va, Pg, Qg)\n```\n\n### 2. Objective function\n\nBuild symbolic expression:\n\n```python\n# Quadratic cost: sum of c2*P^2 + c1*P + c0\nobj = ca.MX(0)\nfor k in range(n_gen):\n    obj += c2[k] * Pg[k]**2 + c1[k] * Pg[k] + c0[k]\n```\n\n### 3. Constraints\n\nCollect constraints in lists with bounds:\n\n```python\ng_expr = []  # Constraint expressions\nlbg = []     # Lower bounds\nubg = []     # Upper bounds\n\n# Equality constraint: g(x) = 0\ng_expr.append(some_expression)\nlbg.append(0.0)\nubg.append(0.0)\n\n# Inequality constraint: g(x) <= limit\ng_expr.append(another_expression)\nlbg.append(-ca.inf)\nubg.append(limit)\n\n# Two-sided: lo <= g(x) <= hi\ng_expr.append(bounded_expression)\nlbg.append(lo)\nubg.append(hi)\n\ng = ca.vertcat(*g_expr)\n```\n\n### 4. Use multiple initializations:\n\n```python\ninitializations = [x0_from_data, x0_flat_start]\nbest_sol = None\n\nfor x0 in initializations:\n    try:\n        sol = solver(x0=x0, lbx=lbx, ubx=ubx, lbg=lbg, ubg=ubg)\n        if best_sol is None or float(sol[\"f\"]) < float(best_sol[\"f\"]):\n            best_sol = sol\n    except Exception:\n        continue\n\nif best_sol is None:\n    raise RuntimeError(\"Solver failed from all initializations\")\n```\n\n**Good initialization strategies:**\n- Data-derived: Use values from input data, clipped to bounds\n- Flat start: Nominal values (e.g., Vm=1.0, Va=0.0)\n- Always enforce known constraints in initial point (e.g., reference angle = 0)\n\n## Extracting solutions\n\n```python\nx_opt = np.array(sol[\"x\"]).flatten()\n\n# Unpack by slicing (must match variable order)\nVm_sol = x_opt[:n_bus]\nVa_sol = x_opt[n_bus:2*n_bus]\nPg_sol = x_opt[2*n_bus:2*n_bus+n_gen]\nQg_sol = x_opt[2*n_bus+n_gen:]\n```\n\n## Power systems patterns\n\n### Per-unit scaling\n\nWork in per-unit internally, convert for output:\n\n```python\nbaseMVA = 100.0\nPg_pu = Pg_MW / baseMVA      # Input conversion\nPg_MW = Pg_pu * baseMVA      # Output conversion\n```\n\nCost functions often expect MW, not per-unit - Stack variables\n    x = ca.vertcat(Vm, Va, Pg, Qg)\n\n    # Objective: minimize generation cost\n    obj = ca.MX(0)\n    for i in range(n_gen):\n        if gen_status[i] == 1:\n            obj += c2[i] * Pg[i]**2 + c1[i] * Pg[i] + c0[i]\n\n    # Constraint expressions and bounds\n    g_expr = []\n    lbg = []\n    ubg = []\n\n    # Reference bus angle\n    g_expr.append(Va[ref_bus])\n    lbg.append(0.0)\n    ubg.append(0.0)\n\n    # Power balance at each bus\n    for i in range(n_bus):\n        # Generator injection\n        Pg_bus = ca.MX(0)\n        Qg_bus = ca.MX(0)\n        for k in gen_at_bus[i]:\n            Pg_bus += Pg[k]\n            Qg_bus += Qg[k]\n\n        # Shunt\n        Psh = Gs[i] * Vm[i]**2\n        Qsh = -Bs[i] * Vm[i]**2\n\n        # Branch flows\n        P_out = ca.MX(0)\n        Q_out = ca.MX(0)\n\n        for k in range(n_branch):\n            if branch_status[k] != 1:\n                continue\n\n            fbus_id = f_bus[k]\n            tbus_id = t_bus[k]\n            i_idx = bus_id_to_idx[fbus_id]\n            j_idx = bus_id_to_idx[tbus_id]\n\n            inv_t = 1.0 / tap[k]\n            inv_t2 = inv_t * inv_t\n\n            if i_idx == i:\n                # Flow from bus i\n                delta_ij = Va[i_idx] - Va[j_idx] - shift[k]\n                c_ij = ca.cos(delta_ij)\n                s_ij = ca.sin(delta_ij)\n\n                P_ij = g[k] * Vm[i_idx]**2 * inv_t2 - Vm[i_idx] * Vm[j_idx] * inv_t * (g[k] * c_ij + b[k] * s_ij)\n                Q_ij = -(b[k] + bc[k] / 2.0) * Vm[i_idx]**2 * inv_t2 - Vm[i_idx] * Vm[j_idx] * inv_t * (g[k] * s_ij - b[k] * c_ij)\n\n                P_out += P_ij\n                Q_out += Q_ij\n\n            if j_idx == i:\n                # Power balance: Gen - Demand - Shunt - Branch_out = 0\n        g_expr.append(Pg_bus - Pd[i] - Psh - P_out)\n        lbg.append(0.0)\n        ubg.append(0.0)\n\n        g_expr.append(Qg_bus - Qd[i] - Qsh - Q_out)\n        lbg.append(0.0)\n        ubg.append(0.0)\n\n    # Stack constraints\n    g = ca.vertcat(*g_expr)\n\n    # Variable bounds\n    Vm_min = Vmin.tolist()\n    Vm_max = Vmax.tolist()\n    Va_min = [-ca.inf] * n_bus\n    Va_max = [ca.inf] * n_bus\n    Va_min[ref_bus] = 0.0\n    Va_max[ref_bus] = 0.0\n\n    Pg_min = []\n    Pg_max = []\n    Qg_min_list = []\n    Qg_max_list = []\n    for i in range(n_gen):\n        if gen_status[i] == 1:\n            Pg_min.append(float(Pmin[i]))\n            Pg_max.append(float(Pmax[i]))\n            Qg_min_list.append(float(Qmin[i]))\n            Qg_max_list.append(float(Qmax[i]))\n        else:\n            Pg_min.append(0.0)\n            Pg_max.append(0.0)\n            Qg_min_list.append(0.0)\n            Qg_max_list.append(0.0)\n\n    lbx = Vm_min + Va_min + Pg_min + Qg_min_list\n    ubx = Vm_max + Va_max + Pg_max + Qg_max_list\n\n    # Create NLP\n    nlp = {'x': x, 'f': obj, 'g': g}\n\n    opts = {\n        'ipopt.print_level': 5,\n        'ipopt.max_iter': 2000,\n        'ipopt.tol': 1e-6,\n        'ipopt.acceptable_tol': 1e-4,\n        'ipopt.mu_strategy': 'adaptive',\n        'ipopt.nlp_scaling_method': 'gradient-based',\n        'print_time': False\n    }\n\n    solver = ca.nlpsol('solver', 'ipopt', nlp, opts)\n\n    # Try multiple initializations\n    initializations = []\n\n    # Init 1: From data\n    Va_init_rad = Va_init_deg * np.pi / 180\n    x0_data = np.concatenate([\n        np.clip(Vm_init, Vmin, Vmax),\n        Va_init_rad,\n        np.clip(Pg_init, Pmin, Pmax),\n        np.clip(Qg_init, Qmin, Qmax)\n    ])\n    initializations.append(('data', x0_data))\n\n    # Solve\n    print(\"Starting AC-OPF optimization with multiple initializations...\")\n    best_sol = None\n    solver_status = \"failed\"\n\n    for init_name, x0 in initializations:\n        print(f\"\\nTrying initialization: {init_name}\")\n        try:\n            sol = solver(x0=x0, lbx=lbx, ubx=ubx, lbg=lbg, ubg=ubg)\n            stats = solver.stats()\n\n            if stats['success'] or stats['return_status'] in ['Solve_Succeeded', 'Solved_To_Acceptable_Level']:\n                print(f\"Success with {init_name} initialization!\")\n                solver_status = \"optimal\"\n                best_sol = sol\n                break\n            else:\n                print(f\"Failed with {init_name}: {stats['return_status']}\")\n                if best_sol is None:\n                    best_sol = sol\n\n        except Exception as e:\n            print(f\"Exception with {init_name}: {e}\")\n            continue\n\n    if best_sol is None:\n        raise RuntimeError(\"All initializations failed\")\n\n    #
